==== file: src/content/guide.ts
== scene: GUIDE
[0di3y2n] ? (p) | A Whorl is a creature that fights for you. Your team consists of the Whorls you take with you. A team can have 4 Whorls.
[01fyq3r] ? (p) | The first Whorl in your lineup is your lead. Your lead walks with you and is the first to enter battle.
[1b061zg] ? (p) | Only one Whorl fights at a time. This is your out Whorl, while the others remain in reserve.
[1tclyq5] ? (p) | Select Switch during a battle to bring a Whorl from reserve into action. Switching is swift. The incoming Whorl waits less time than it would after an Attack. Higher CHA reduces this waiting period even further.
[1hryecb] ? (p) | A Whorl that is rooted or taunted cannot be switched out.
[0p69sbf] ? (p) | A Whorl with 0 HP is knocked out. The game calls this down or a KO. It cannot fight again in that battle. Pick the next Whorl to send out.
[0fmxv0h] ? (p) | Battles begin with full HP. A KO'd Whorl is fine when the battle ends.
[0c5xine] ? (p) | If your entire team is defeated, you lose the battle. You will lose 20% of your cowries and respawn at the last grotto you visited. Grottos are the shops found in every town.
[176mcbj] ? (p) | When your team is full, any new Whorls you catch are sent to the Midden. You can access the Midden from any grotto, where you can swap Whorls in and out of your active team.
[1y8gcuc] ? (p) | Press X to open the menu, select Team, choose a Whorl, then pick Make lead.
[1j7maix] ? (p) | Each Whorl has seven stats. A stat is a number. It shows how good the Whorl is at one thing.
[0h7tcio] ? (s) | HP is health. A Whorl with 0 HP is knocked out. Max HP is the most health it can have.
[1uzujth] ? (s) | ATK makes physical hits stronger.
[0f7jdzg] ? (s) | MGK boosts magic attacks.
[1uwbntp] ? (s) | DEF makes physical hits do less damage.
[0pgy7qb] ? (s) | RES makes magic hits hurt less.
[0fk9rgx] ? (s) | Speed is AGI. A Whorl with more AGI takes more turns.
[1glt27d] ? (s) | CHA helps a horn catch Whorls. It also reduces the wait time after using a Switch. Some moves use CHA too.
[1p27oiq] ? (p) | Every species is strong in different ways. One might have more ATK. Another might have more DEF. They also have different moves, habits, and types.
[1n34rdy] ? (p) | All species get the same number of stat points. They use them in different ways. A fast species has less DEF. A tough species gets fewer turns. A conjoined Whorl gets half its points from each of the two old Whorls.
[1ig77ux] ? (p) | This picture shows your lead's stats. The stats go up when a Whorl levels up. You can find things later that raise them a little more.
[0rlbygh] ? (p) | Many texts use the % sign. It means parts of 100. 50% is half. 25% is one quarter. 10% is one tenth.
[0kkctb7] ? (p) | Every Whorl has a simple Attack. It is either physical or magic. Physical Attacks use ATK. Magic Attacks use MGK.
[034wdnd] ? (p) | The move's text tells you which stat it uses. Hitting for 110% ATK is a physical hit. Hitting for 90% MGK is a magic hit.
[022p2rp] ? (p) | DEF and RES make hits hurt less. Say a hit has 100 power. It does 70 damage to a Whorl with 42 DEF. It does 50 damage to a Whorl with 100 DEF.
[1pt8wdo] ? (p) | DEF works best against physical Whorls. RES works best against magic Whorls.
[15x7n0o] ? (p) | Press X to see stats. Tap Team, pick the Whorl, then tap Look.
[1vkqp2i] ? (p) | In combat, a Whorl pauses after taking an action. Higher AGI reduces this pause. Thus, a quicker Whorl gains additional turns.
[1nvczk0] ? (p) | Example: Your Whorl has 69 AGI, while the foe has 51. Throughout the battle, your Whorl gains roughly 9 turns for every 8 the foe gets.
[01q6u95] ? (p) | Typically, turns occur in close succession, meaning both sides choose their actions simultaneously. Neither party can see the other's selection at that moment.
[108apb4] ? (p) | Actions resolve in order. The Whorl whose turn comes first goes first. The top line indicates who moves first.
[130lgnl] ? (p) | Guard and Switch always go first. Quick moves follow. Late moves wait until the foe has acted.
[0l98kh2] ? (p) | Guess the foe's move. If a big hit is coming, Guard. If the foe Guards, use a dash. If they use TIDE, switch to a Whorl resistant to it.
[195jtju] ? (p) | Sometimes one side moves much sooner. That side picks alone and moves. So a fast Whorl can move twice before a slow one moves once. The top line shows when this happens.
[09hov8o] ? (p) | Some actions have short waits. A Whorl waits 80% as long after Guard as after Attack. The incoming Whorl waits about 70%. More CHA shortens this wait. The Switch list shows the wait for each Whorl.
[0hkypy1] ? (p) | Slow 1 delays the Whorl's next turn. It waits 30% of a turn more. Slow 2 does this for its next 2 turns.
[0ybdxjp] ? (p) | Haste operates in the opposite manner, causing each subsequent turn to arrive 30% of a turn earlier.
[0h6ajqx] ? (p) | A slowed foe often goes last. Slow it twice, and you may get a turn.
[16pmtcu] ? (p) | Some text says the Whorl's next turn comes 40% sooner. This means the Whorl waits less before it moves again. The wait is about 40% shorter.
[0uu626p] ? (p) | Some moves change the field for a few rounds. Sea Fog and High Tide are examples. The field affects both sides, so it can help the foe too.
[0jjpyl2] ? (p) | A wind-up move lands later. A ! appears over the Whorl while winding up. The top line shows how many moves you get first.
[01v7l2b] ? (p) | You can stop a foe's wind-up. Stun, Silence, Sleep, or Taunt cuts it off. Forcing the foe out also stops it.
[17tbnqk] ? (p) | You can also switch to a Whorl that takes less damage from the move. An Unstoppable wind-up cannot be interrupted.
[1v0fh4o] ? (p) | A round consists of one turn per side. The field tracks the rounds, as does any prolonged battle.
[00cy5q9] ? (title) | Attack, moves, and Guard
[0qb4bnu] ? (p) | On your turn, choose one action: Attack, use a move, Guard, or Switch.
[1ce6vgm] ? (p) | Attack is your Whorl's standard strike. You can use it every turn, and it never depletes. Since it has no type, it deals equal damage to all types.
[1drxrtt] ? (p) | Attacks can strike more than once. A habit that functions after an Attack still works once for the entire Attack. A habit that mentions hits applies to each hit.
[0vqi9ja] ? (p) | Each Whorl has four moves. These moves deal more damage or provide special effects. Review the move's description before selecting it to understand its effect.
[0tplqaq] ? (p) | Hits for 110% ATK means a hit with 1.1 times the Whorl's ATK. Slow 1 means the foe is slowed for 1 turn.
[0bpzu3g] ? (p) | After using a move, it must recharge. This recharge period is its cooldown, and the menu displays the remaining turns.
[0zgjawx] ? (p) | Flatten's cooldown is 3. Squall uses it. It rests for Squall's next 3 turns. It's ready on turn 4.
[0dl84ib] ? (p) | Cooldowns count your side's turns. They decrease while the Whorl waits in reserve.
[1mn8yxm] ? (p) | Moves matching a Whorl's own type deal 20% more damage. Squall is a TIDE Whorl, so its TIDE moves deal 20% more damage.
[1cm5at0] ? (p) | Some moves splash damage. The primary target takes full damage, while each foe in reserve takes a portion. The move description specifies the percentage.
[01k343e] ? (p) | Splash 35% means each foe in reserve takes 35% of the damage. A small splash is called a spray. Splash 100% damages the entire enemy team equally.
[175jrs7] ? (p) | Guard reduces the damage your Whorl takes by half. It lasts until your Whorl's next turn. A Guard always acts first in a round.
[1fdnddl] ? (p) | Guard blocks a larger share of a projectile but does not block a dash at all.
